# Las 1000 palabras de Word Beat

## Verbos en los 11 tiempos verbales

Cada verbo con sujeto I y she, en afirmativa, negativa y pregunta, con su traducción. En la app, el conjugador de cada palabra muestra los 6 sujetos.

### have (tener / haber)

| Tiempo | Nivel | Afirmativa | Negativa | Pregunta |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Presente simple | Básico | I have breakfast every day.<br>_Yo desayuno todos los días._ | She doesn't have breakfast every day.<br>_Ella no desayuna todos los días._ | Does she have breakfast every day?<br>_¿Ella desayuna todos los días?_ |
| Pasado simple | Básico | I had breakfast yesterday.<br>_Yo desayuné ayer._ | She didn't have breakfast yesterday.<br>_Ella no desayunó ayer._ | Did she have breakfast yesterday?<br>_¿Ella desayunó ayer?_ |
| Presente perfecto | Básico | I have had breakfast before.<br>_Yo he desayunado antes._ | She hasn't had breakfast before.<br>_Ella no ha desayunado antes._ | Has she had breakfast before?<br>_¿Ella ha desayunado antes?_ |
| Presente continuo | Básico | I am having breakfast right now.<br>_Yo estoy desayunando ahora mismo._ | She isn't having breakfast right now.<br>_Ella no está desayunando ahora mismo._ | Is she having breakfast right now?<br>_¿Ella está desayunando ahora mismo?_ |
| Futuro (will) | Básico | I will have breakfast tomorrow.<br>_Yo desayunaré mañana._ | She won't have breakfast tomorrow.<br>_Ella no desayunará mañana._ | Will she have breakfast tomorrow?<br>_¿Ella desayunará mañana?_ |
| Pasado continuo | Intermedio | I was having breakfast at eight last night.<br>_Yo estaba desayunando anoche a las ocho._ | She wasn't having breakfast at eight last night.<br>_Ella no estaba desayunando anoche a las ocho._ | Was she having breakfast at eight last night?<br>_¿Ella estaba desayunando anoche a las ocho?_ |
| Futuro (going to) | Intermedio | I am going to have breakfast next week.<br>_Yo voy a desayunar la próxima semana._ | She isn't going to have breakfast next week.<br>_Ella no va a desayunar la próxima semana._ | Is she going to have breakfast next week?<br>_¿Ella va a desayunar la próxima semana?_ |
| Condicional | Avanzado | I would have breakfast if possible.<br>_Yo desayunaría si fuera posible._ | She wouldn't have breakfast if possible.<br>_Ella no desayunaría si fuera posible._ | Would she have breakfast if possible?<br>_¿Ella desayunaría si fuera posible?_ |
| Pasado perfecto | Avanzado | I had had breakfast before the party.<br>_Yo había desayunado antes de la fiesta._ | She hadn't had breakfast before the party.<br>_Ella no había desayunado antes de la fiesta._ | Had she had breakfast before the party?<br>_¿Ella había desayunado antes de la fiesta?_ |
| Futuro continuo | Avanzado | I will be having breakfast at this time tomorrow.<br>_Yo estaré desayunando mañana a esta hora._ | She won't be having breakfast at this time tomorrow.<br>_Ella no estará desayunando mañana a esta hora._ | Will she be having breakfast at this time tomorrow?<br>_¿Ella estará desayunando mañana a esta hora?_ |
| Presente perfecto continuo | Avanzado | I have been having breakfast all day.<br>_Yo he estado desayunando todo el día._ | She hasn't been having breakfast all day.<br>_Ella no ha estado desayunando todo el día._ | Has she been having breakfast all day?<br>_¿Ella ha estado desayunando todo el día?_ |

### do (hacer (actividad) / auxiliar de preguntas)

| Tiempo | Nivel | Afirmativa | Negativa | Pregunta |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Presente simple | Básico | I do the homework every day.<br>_Yo hago la tarea todos los días._ | She doesn't do the homework every day.<br>_Ella no hace la tarea todos los días._ | Does she do the homework every day?<br>_¿Ella hace la tarea todos los días?_ |
| Pasado simple | Básico | I did the homework yesterday.<br>_Yo hice la tarea ayer._ | She didn't do the homework yesterday.<br>_Ella no hizo la tarea ayer._ | Did she do the homework yesterday?<br>_¿Ella hizo la tarea ayer?_ |
| Presente perfecto | Básico | I have done the homework before.<br>_Yo he hecho la tarea antes._ | She hasn't done the homework before.<br>_Ella no ha hecho la tarea antes._ | Has she done the homework before?<br>_¿Ella ha hecho la tarea antes?_ |
| Presente continuo | Básico | I am doing the homework right now.<br>_Yo estoy haciendo la tarea ahora mismo._ | She isn't doing the homework right now.<br>_Ella no está haciendo la tarea ahora mismo._ | Is she doing the homework right now?<br>_¿Ella está haciendo la tarea ahora mismo?_ |
| Futuro (will) | Básico | I will do the homework tomorrow.<br>_Yo haré la tarea mañana._ | She won't do the homework tomorrow.<br>_Ella no hará la tarea mañana._ | Will she do the homework tomorrow?<br>_¿Ella hará la tarea mañana?_ |
| Pasado continuo | Intermedio | I was doing the homework at eight last night.<br>_Yo estaba haciendo la tarea anoche a las ocho._ | She wasn't doing the homework at eight last night.<br>_Ella no estaba haciendo la tarea anoche a las ocho._ | Was she doing the homework at eight last night?<br>_¿Ella estaba haciendo la tarea anoche a las ocho?_ |
| Futuro (going to) | Intermedio | I am going to do the homework next week.<br>_Yo voy a hacer la tarea la próxima semana._ | She isn't going to do the homework next week.<br>_Ella no va a hacer la tarea la próxima semana._ | Is she going to do the homework next week?<br>_¿Ella va a hacer la tarea la próxima semana?_ |
| Condicional | Avanzado | I would do the homework if possible.<br>_Yo haría la tarea si fuera posible._ | She wouldn't do the homework if possible.<br>_Ella no haría la tarea si fuera posible._ | Would she do the homework if possible?<br>_¿Ella haría la tarea si fuera posible?_ |
| Pasado perfecto | Avanzado | I had done the homework before the party.<br>_Yo había hecho la tarea antes de la fiesta._ | She hadn't done the homework before the party.<br>_Ella no había hecho la tarea antes de la fiesta._ | Had she done the homework before the party?<br>_¿Ella había hecho la tarea antes de la fiesta?_ |
| Futuro continuo | Avanzado | I will be doing the homework at this time tomorrow.<br>_Yo estaré haciendo la tarea mañana a esta hora._ | She won't be doing the homework at this time tomorrow.<br>_Ella no estará haciendo la tarea mañana a esta hora._ | Will she be doing the homework at this time tomorrow?<br>_¿Ella estará haciendo la tarea mañana a esta hora?_ |
| Presente perfecto continuo | Avanzado | I have been doing the homework all day.<br>_Yo he estado haciendo la tarea todo el día._ | She hasn't been doing the homework all day.<br>_Ella no ha estado haciendo la tarea todo el día._ | Has she been doing the homework all day?<br>_¿Ella ha estado haciendo la tarea todo el día?_ |

### know (saber / conocer)

| Tiempo | Nivel | Afirmativa | Negativa | Pregunta |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Presente simple | Básico | I know the answer.<br>_Yo sé la respuesta._ | She doesn't know the answer.<br>_Ella no sabe la respuesta._ | Does she know the answer?<br>_¿Ella sabe la respuesta?_ |
| Pasado simple | Básico | I knew the answer last year.<br>_Yo supe la respuesta el año pasado._ | She didn't know the answer last year.<br>_Ella no supo la respuesta el año pasado._ | Did she know the answer last year?<br>_¿Ella supo la respuesta el año pasado?_ |
| Presente perfecto | Básico | I have known the answer for a long time.<br>_Yo he sabido la respuesta desde hace mucho tiempo._ | She hasn't known the answer for a long time.<br>_Ella no ha sabido la respuesta desde hace mucho tiempo._ | Has she known the answer for a long time?<br>_¿Ella ha sabido la respuesta desde hace mucho tiempo?_ |
| Futuro (will) | Básico | I will know the answer tomorrow.<br>_Yo sabré la respuesta mañana._ | She won't know the answer tomorrow.<br>_Ella no sabrá la respuesta mañana._ | Will she know the answer tomorrow?<br>_¿Ella sabrá la respuesta mañana?_ |
| Futuro (going to) | Intermedio | I am going to know the answer next week.<br>_Yo voy a saber la respuesta la próxima semana._ | She isn't going to know the answer next week.<br>_Ella no va a saber la respuesta la próxima semana._ | Is she going to know the answer next week?<br>_¿Ella va a saber la respuesta la próxima semana?_ |
| Condicional | Avanzado | I would know the answer if possible.<br>_Yo sabría la respuesta si fuera posible._ | She wouldn't know the answer if possible.<br>_Ella no sabría la respuesta si fuera posible._ | Would she know the answer if possible?<br>_¿Ella sabría la respuesta si fuera posible?_ |
